
Every code table in Chapter 2C, and the artifacts that represent it.
**799 tables.**

### How the links work

The text of every link is the artifact's **canonical URL on THO** — that is its identifier, and it does not change. Where the link *goes* depends on who holds the artifact:

- If **this guide** authors it, the link opens the draft in this guide. That is the copy under review, so it is the one you want.
- If this guide authors nothing, the link opens the **artifact on THO**.
- If neither holds it, the canonical is shown as plain text.

The marker after each link says which of the three applies, so clicking never surprises you.

The **Concept domain** column is the exception, and carries no such marker. Its links always open this guide, because this guide republishes the whole `conceptdomains` code system at THO's canonical — a marker saying so on every row would say nothing. The marker there answers a different question: **already in THO** for a concept domain THO publishes today, **added by this guide** for one this guide introduces.

Where the [per-table ledger](table-ledger.html) has a section for a table, a **ledger** link next to the table number opens it. 455 of the 799 tables have one; the remaining 344 have nothing recorded against them there.

The **Type** column is how Chapter 2C itself types the table. `undefined` is the chapter's own word for a table it leaves untyped, so it is shown rather than left blank.

### What "in THO" means here

Every claim on this page about what THO holds is checked against a local snapshot of the **`hl7.terminology.r5` package, version 7.3.0** (published 2026-07-27), not against a live server. Inside that package the `conceptdomains` code system is at **3.0.0** and `v2-tables` is at **7.0.0** — resource versions move independently of the package version.

Those are **artifact** versions, which move per resource and are not the version of the THO release that carries them. `conceptdomains` is at 3.0.0 in THO; this guide changes it, so its artifact version increments to **4.0.0**. That says nothing about which THO release would publish it, and no row on this page names a version for an individual concept.

### What the shading means

For most tables the artifact exists **both here and in THO** — this guide is a redraft of something THO already carries. That is the norm and it is left unshaded. Shading marks only the rows that depart from what their route would predict:

<p>
<span class="ti-key" style="background:#fff4e0"></span><strong>New to THO</strong> (7) &nbsp;&nbsp;
<span class="ti-key" style="background:#eaf1fb"></span><strong>In THO, not here</strong> (3) &nbsp;&nbsp;
<span class="ti-key" style="background:#fdecec"></span><strong>Dangling in THO</strong> (4) &nbsp;&nbsp;
</p>

Every shaded row repeats its reason in the Notes column, so the page reads correctly without the colours.

<a id="coverage-anomalies"></a>

#### The tables whose artifact is missing, unexpected, or cannot expand

**19 tables** qualify.

These are **coverage** anomalies — the artifact is not where this table's route predicts it would be. That is a different question from whether a table's *content* differs from THO, or whether it carries an open register item; for those, see [Tables with a difference from THO or an open item](table-ledger.html#content-and-open-items), which lists a considerably larger set. A table can appear on both lists, on one, or on neither.

| Table | Name | Why |
|---|---|---|
| [0005](#tbl-0005) | Race | THO publishes a code system and value set at this canonical that this guide does not author. THO's code system is retired. |
| [0010](#tbl-0010) | Physician ID | No concept domain, no code system and no value set — here or in THO. Whether this table should become a concept domain is an open question. |
| [0133](#tbl-0133) | Procedure practitioner identifier code type | THO publishes a code system and value set at this canonical that this guide does not author. |
| [0188](#tbl-0188) | Operator ID | No concept domain, no code system and no value set — here or in THO. Whether this table should become a concept domain is an open question. |

Whether this table should become a concept domain is an open question. Routed as concept-domain-only, but the chapter names no domain. |

### Tables THO still indexes that Chapter 2C no longer carries

THO's `v2-tables` holds **39 entries that are not tables of V2.9.1 Chapter 2C** and so have no row above. They are listed because of what their `vocab-domain` property points at: **35 of them name a concept domain that `conceptdomains` does not define.** A reader following that property finds nothing.

Only three are typed *no longer used*, so the index does not otherwise mark them as retired. Registered for TSMG as `esc-v2tables-domain-gaps`.
